{"file_path": "C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/research/2026-10-13-spreeder-reconstruction-spec.md", "content": "---\ntitle: Spreeder reconstruction spec (visual + behaviour)\ndate: 2026-10-13\nsources:\n  - Live capture of spreeder.ereflect.com in Joshua's logged-in Chrome (account \"mk\"), 1536x695 viewport, 2026-10-13\n  - Static code: main.21893fdef5a7593c.js + 219 lazy chunks from resources.ereflect.com/Spreeder/webApp/ (REA analyze-javascript-application, 222 files, 2804 modules, 0 parse failures)\nsupersedes: visual guesses in brainstorms/2026-10-12-reading-center-build-spec.md (that spec said \"use the app's design tokens\"; Joshua wants Spreeder matched identically)\ntarget: dev/ai-os-projects/reading-center (standalone first, LSAT U later)\n---\n\n# Spreeder reconstruction spec\n\nObserved = seen live or read in shipped code. Inferred = marked. Brand assets (Spreeder logo, cover art,\neReflect icon fonts `iconNewui`/`iconSpreed`) are **not** copied; equivalents are named.\n\n## 1. Global\n\n- UI font **Poppins** (Google Fonts, OFL). Reading text uses the chosen reading font (default Arial).\n- Icons: reader header uses **Ionicons** (MIT): `arrow-back-outline`, `search`, `expand-outline`, `bookmark`, `settings`.\n  Dialogs/menus use **Material Icons** (Apache 2.0): `close`, `arrow_drop_down`, `more_vert`, `navigate_next`, `keyboard_arrow_left/right`.\n  Speed gauge / view-text / keyboard / list glyphs are eReflect's own font: substitute Ionicons `speedometer`, `reader`/`list`, `keypad`.\n- Themes (whole app, not just reader): Light, Dark, High Contrast, System.\n\n| Token | Light | Dark | High Contrast |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| page bg | #ffffff | #000000 | #121212 |\n| reading surface | #ffffff | #000000 | #1e1e1e |\n| primary text | #424242 (chrome), #000 (reading) | #ffffff | #ffffff |\n| secondary text | #757575 | #ffffff | #e0e0e0 |\n| card (profile) | n/c | #252525 | inferred #242424 |\n| dialog bg | #ffffff | #000000 | #242424, border .8px #6c6c6c |\n| dialog shadow | rgba(0,0,0,.2) 0 11px 15px -7px … | rgba(255,255,255,.4) -1px 4px 29px 0 | same as dark |\n| backdrop | rgba(0,0,0,.55) | rgba(110,118,125,.4) | rgba(110,118,125,.4) |\n| outline borders | #cccccc | #cccccc (dropdown), #444 (secondary btn) | #444444 |\n| bottom bar | rgba(255,255,255,.85), top .8px #ccc | rgba(0,0,0,.8), top .8px #121313 | #1a1a1a, top .8px #6c6c6c |\n| unhighlighted words (Highlight) | #cbcbcb | #444 | #444 |\n| accent green (active tab, Save) | #34ad07 | #34ad07 text, ink #71f141 @15% | — |\n| accent blue (speed/view) | #0a85ff | #0a85ff | — |\n| slider / toggle blue | #3e92d7 knob, track tint | same | same |\n\n## 2. Shell (library / profile)\n\n- Left sidenav 230px, bg rgba(0,0,0,.85) (dark). Hamburger top-left, product logo (substitute: wordmark \"Reading Center\").\n- \"Add Text\" button 189x55 at (20,70): bg #073555, text #009cdb Poppins 14/500, radius 12, blue ⊕ icon left. Opens menu: Paste Text, Import Document, AI Book Summary, AI Generate (icons left, dark panel #252525-ish).\n- Nav rows 56px apart, icon x44, label x71, Poppins 14/500 white: Spreed (book icon), Tags (tag), Courses (mortarboard), Profile (person).\n- Active row: 189x52 pill radius 8, bg #1a3b13, label/icon #3aab1f, 4px green left bar. Hover: bg #2c2c2c-ish.\n- Top bar right: book-search icon, search box 232x32 bg #252525 radius 8 \".8px\" border, placeholder \"Searching N books..\", magnifier; apps grid; account icon + caret.\n\n## 3. Library (/library)\n\n- Content x=250. Shelves: **Recent**; **Added By Me** `View All >`; **In Progress** `View All >`. Heading Poppins bold ~18px.\n- Card: cover 148x209 radius ~4 (user text: solid #009cdb tile with white document glyph). Title Poppins 13 white 2 lines, author grey small, ⋮ (more_vert) right of title.\n- ⋮ menu: View/Location (eye), Edit Image (image), Edit Text (edit), Tag (tag), Delete (trash), Hide from Recent (eye-off). Dark panel, Poppins 15.\n- Paste Text: full-screen \"Add To Library\" (Poppins 24), X top-right, underline field \"Enter Title\", bordered box \"Add Text Here\", bottom-right Cancel (outlined) + Save (disabled until valid).\n\n## 4. Reader (/spreed)\n\n- Header 56px: back arrow 24px at (32,14) + \"Back\" Poppins 14/500 ls .28px; centred title Poppins 18/400 ls .72px; right icons 26px at x 1405/1450/1496 (expand, bookmark, settings); e-Reader adds search at x 1359.\n- Reading card: width 72% (100% under 1380px), radius 10, padding 20px 0, full height, canvas inside. Stage padding 20 left/right.\n- Transport (hidden while playing): prev ◁ and next ▷ outlined triangles 50x50 at ±100px of centre, circle play ~60px. Hover: light grey circle behind.\n- Clicking the reading area toggles play/pause. Window blur pauses. Resize pauses and relays out.\n- Bottom bar 40px: keyboard button left (~x251); centre two 80x30 buttons radius 4 border .8px: gauge icon + WPM, list icon + \"View\", colour #0a85ff Poppins 13/500; right \"#readingTimespan\" Poppins 14/400 `${pct}% - ${time} remaining`.\n- Speed change toast: \"N WPM\" pill bg #323232 white 14px padding 8 radius 4, top 10px centred, fades in .5s, holds 2s, fades out 2s.\n\n### Text model (shipped code)\n- Whitespace collapsed to single spaces; em dash → \"-\"; **no paragraphs**.\n- Sections: sentences (regex `[^.!?]+([.!?\"”]+|$)`) accumulated until ~2000 chars; a sentence that would push past 2400 is split at a word boundary. Sentence ends after \"Dr/Mr/Mrs/Ms\" or a single letter do not close a section.\n- Words = split(\" \"); id 1-based; charPosition.\n- Progress % = round((sectionIndex + posInSection/sectionLen) / sections * 100) (1 when on the last word of a section).\n- Remaining = time from current word to end: \"N hour(s) and M minute(s)\", \"Less than a minute\".\n\n### Layout (Serial / Flow / Highlight / Scroll)\n- Words are measured in the font; a line takes words while `x + width + space <= stageWidth - 40` **and** words < Words Per Line.\n- Serial: slide = Number of Lines lines, block vertically centred; Center Text centres each line, else x=20.\n- Flow: forced 1 line, spacing 1, left-aligned x=20; each chunk revealed by a bg-coloured mask sweeping left→right across the window over the chunk's duration (blank when paused at chunk start).\n- Highlight: page = floor((height-20)/fontSize) lines, left x=20, all visible in unhighlighted colour; current group (Number of Lines lines) drawn in font colour, not bold.\n- Scroll: same page, only current group visible at its own y; previous group hidden; new page starts at top.\n- Font size clamp 8..74. Line spacing multiplies fontSize.\n\n### Timing (shipped code)\n- Section total T = round(words / wpm * 60000). Chunks n. Even delay = T / (n - 1 + f), last = even*f where f = min(lastChunkLines / numberOfLines, 1).\n- Speed Variability: delay = round(T/n) * (chunkChars + (notLast?1:0)) / (sectionChars/n).\n- Punctuation Pauses: delay *= 2 if chunk text contains any of `. ? ! : ; - – ,` (any group size).\n- WPM clamp 50..5000, step 10 (keys ↑/+ and ↓/-), rounded to 10.\n\n### Dialogs\n- Preferences: 550x599 centred, radius 15, padding 14. Title \"<first name>'s preferences\" Poppins 24/400 (dark white / light #424242), close X.\n  - Tabs Spreed/Appearance/Advanced Poppins 14 (active 500 #34ad07 on pill 100x30 radius 99 bg accent @15%; idle #757575 light / white dark).\n  - Spreed: \"Reading Mode:\" 13/400 ls .52px + dropdown (93x36, border .8 #ccc, radius 4, \"Serial\" 14/500 + arrow_drop_down). Sliders with tick dots, label below-left 12/400, value below-right. Serial: Number of Lines, Words Per Line, Center Text toggle. Flow/Scroll: Words Per Line. Highlight: Words Per Line, Number of Lines.\n  - Appearance: Theme dropdown; Font dropdown (wide, filled #eee in light) + Size dropdown; large \"Sample Text\" centred in chosen font/size.\n  - Advanced: Punctuation Pauses (default on) \"Slight pause at end of sentences and punctuation marks. Only works for group size of 1.\"; Speed Variability (default off) \"Slow down for larger chunks and speed up for smaller chunks so average wpm stays the same\". White round \"?\" icons.\n  - e-Reader mode: no tabs; Reading Mode, Theme, Font+Size, Sample Text, Line Spacing slider (1.0–2.0, 10 ticks).\n  - Restore Defaults: bottom-left, 149x38 outlined. Only X closes (Escape does not).\n  - Dropdown menus: white (light) / #252525 (dark) panel, items Poppins 15, hover grey.\n- Reading Speed (WPM): 528x363, radius 15. Title Poppins 24 #424242. Value 45px/400 #424242 centred. Left column -10 above -100, right column +10 above +100: 75x55, radius 15, bg #d5e7fb, text #0a85ff 18/500. Footer right: Cancel (outlined #ccc) + Save (bg #34ad07 white).\n- View: full screen sheet. Title Poppins ~24 #424242 top-left, X top-right. Chip \"User added\" grey pill. Pill search \"Search this text...\" top-right; on Enter shows X, \"i of n\", ▼ ▲; whole-word match; current hit #fab95d, others #fae55d, radius 4. Text Poppins 16/400 #212121 lh 33; current word outlined 1.6px #34ad07 radius 5; click word to jump. Grey round play button bottom-centre. Thin rule then footer \"3 minutes to go - 200 WPM\" Poppins 14 #757575.\n- Bookmarks: dialog ~850 wide radius 15; title \"Bookmarks\"; empty state grey 150px circle with list glyph, \"No Bookmarks Yet\" Poppins ~28 #616161, text \"To create a bookmark, simply pause Spreeder while you're reading and long press or click on any word.\" Bookmark rows have optional notes (\"Enter Notes (Optional)...\", \"Go to location\") — from code, not seen filled.\n- Keyboard Shortcuts popover: white card radius ~12 with pointer to the keyboard button; grey keycaps with bottom shadow: ↑ / ↓ or + - \"increase or decrease speed\"; P or SPACE BAR \"pause / resume\"; → \"section forward\", R \"restart\"; ← \"section back\", S \"settings\".\n- Finish: \"You have successfully completed <strong>title</strong>. It will now be moved to your finished section on the Home Screen.\"\n\n### e-Reader\n- Column ~734px centred, reading font/size, lh = font size × spacing; text fills page height and clips. Hover word → #3e92d7.\n- Bottom: ion-range full width (bar 4px rgba(100,100,100,.4), active + knob 25px #3e92d7), \"n / N\" #424242cc, chevrons.\n- Search: right panel card \"Search\" + X; underline field \"Search in this book\"; \"N results in book\"; results grouped \"Page p (k results)\" with snippet, match bold; selected group bg light blue; matches highlighted #ffff00 in text.\n\n## 5. Profile (/profile)\n\n- Header card #252525 radius 15: avatar circle, \"<name>'s Spreeder\" Poppins 25/300, pill \"✦ 0 AI Credits\"; tabs Dashboard / Stats (active = green pill) / Options ▾ / share.\n- Dashboard: 2x2 cards #252525 radius 15: Vital Stats (Current Speed, Target Speed speedometer dials), Reading Focus (↻ 30 Days ▾), Training Time (↻ 6 months ▾), Reading Speed (↻ 30 Days ▾). Empty: \"Nothing just yet. Your results will appear here later 🙂\".\n- Stats: \"Reading Speed ▾\" metric + \"30 Days ▾\"; empty: dark circle with bar-chart tile, \"Not Enough Data\", \"We don't have enough data to display your stats yet. Use the app for a while and come back soon.\"\n- Options menu: Theme: Dark ▸, Edit Profile, Goals, Settings, Sign Out.\n- Goals dialog (black, radius 15): slider; \"Reading Speed Goal (WPM):\" bold + value right; text \"Please enter your goal reading speed in words per minute (WPM). An average person reads at 200 words per minute. We recommend starting with a goal of 600 WPM.\"; Cancel, Recommend, Set (green).\n- Recommend → full-screen \"Goal Recommendation Test\": blue checkered flag, \"Recommend Goals\" (blue ~40px), \"Please complete the following test and Spreeder will recommend your ideal goals.\", green Start. (Test content not inspected; would record results.)\n\n## 6. Not reproduced / out of scope (decide later)\n- 20,504-book catalogue, AI Book Summary / AI Generate (credits), Import Document parsing, Courses (empty for this account), eReflect app grid, share, Wordela word actions in e-Reader (\"Add to Wordela Wordlist\", \"Look Up Word\").\n"}

\"ionicons/dist/svg/person-circle.svg?raw\";\nimport play from \"ionicons/dist/svg/play.svg?raw\";\nimport refresh from \"ionicons/dist/svg/refresh.svg?raw\";\nimport shareSocial from \"ionicons/dist/svg/share-social.svg?raw\";\nimport flag from \"ionicons/dist/svg/flag.svg?raw\";\nimport statsChart from \"ionicons/dist/svg/stats-chart.svg?raw\";\nimport sparkles from \"ionicons/dist/svg/sparkles.svg?raw\";\nimport copy from \"ionicons/dist/svg/copy.svg?raw\";\nimport cloudUpload from \"ionicons/dist/svg/cloud-upload.svg?raw\";\nimport logOut from \"ionicons/dist/svg/log-out.svg?raw\";\nimport close from \"ionicons/dist/svg/close.svg?raw\";\nimport menu from \"ionicons/dist/svg/menu.svg?raw\";\n\n/**\n * Ionicons (MIT), the icon set Spreeder's reader header uses. Spreeder's own\n * icon font (gauge, view-text, keyboard) is substituted with the nearest\n * Ionicon; see research/2026-10-13-spreeder-reconstruction-spec.md section 1.\n */\nconst ICONS = {\n  arrowBack, search, expand, contract, bookmark, settings, speedometer, list, keypad, book, pricetag,\n  school, person, addCircle, documentText, eye, eyeOff, image, create, trash, apps, personCircle, play,\n  refresh, shareSocial, flag, statsChart, sparkles, copy, cloudUpload, logOut, close, menu,\n} as const;\n\nexport type IconName = keyof typeof ICONS;\n\nexport function Icon({ name, size = 24, className = \"\", style }: { name: IconName; size?: number; className?: string; style?: React.CSSProperties }): React.ReactElement {\n  return (\n    <span\n      aria-hidden\n      className={`rc-icon inline-flex shrink-0 ${className}`}\n      style={{ width: size, height: size, ...style }}\n      dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: ICONS[name] }}\n    />\n  );\n}\n\n/** Material Icons ligature (Apache 2.0), used by Spreeder's dialogs and menus. */\nexport function MIcon({ name, size = 24, className = \"\", style }: { name: string; size?: number; className?: string; style?: React.CSSProperties }): React.ReactElement {\n  return (\n    <span aria-hidden className={`material-icons select-none ${className}`} style={{ fontSize: size, width: size, height: size, lineHeight: 1, ...style }}>\n      {name}\n    </span>\n  );\n}\n"}

+ s.length < 2000) {\n      acc += s;\n    } else if (acc.length + s.length > 2400) {\n      const room = Math.max(0, 2400 - acc.length);\n      const head = s.match(new RegExp(\"^.{0,\" + room + \"}[^\\\\s]+\", \"s\"));\n      if (head && head.length > 0) {\n        let part = head[0];\n        if (part.length > acc.length + 2600) part = part.substr(0, 2600 - acc.length);\n        acc += else {\n      acc += s;\n      let close = true;\n      if (i + 1 < sentences.length) {\n        if (sentences[i + 1].charAt(0).replace(/\\s+/g, \"\").length > 0) close = false;\n        if (close) function evenChunkDelays(total: number, count: number, lastLines: number, numberOfLines: number): { even: number; last: number } {\n  if (count <= 1) return { even: total, last: total };\n  const f = Math.min(Math.max(lastLines / numberOfLines, 0), 1);\n  const even = total / (count - 1 + f);\n  return { even, last: even * f };\n}\n\n/** Spreeder computeTotalTimeBySection: word-count chunks of {"file_path": "C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os-projects/reading-center/src/features/reading-center/lib/spreeder.test.ts", "content": "import { describe, expect, it } from \"vitest\";\nimport { SEED_PASSAGE } from \"../data/seed\";\nimport {\n  breakToSections,\n  buildLines,\n  buildTextModel,\n  chunkDelays,\n  evenChunkDelays,\n  formatDuration,\n  highlightPages,\n  normalizeText,\n  progressPercent,\n  remainingMs,\n  sectionTime,\n  serialChunks,\n} from \"./spreeder\";\n\nconst mono = (t: string) => t.length * 10; // 10px per character, space included\n\ndescribe(\"text model\", () => {\n  it(\"collapses paragraphs and swaps em dashes, as Spreeder does\", () => {\n    expect(normalizeText(\"One.\\n\\nTwo—three  four\")).toBe(\"One. Two-three four\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"keeps sentences under 2000 characters together\", () => {\n    expect(breakToSections(\"First one. Second one. Third one.\")).toEqual([\"First one. Second one. Third one.\"]);\n  });\n\n  it(\"does not close a section after Mr. or a single initial\", () => {\n    const long = \"x\".repeat(1990);\n    const s = breakToSections(`${long} said Mr. Smith. Next.`);\n    expect(s[0]).toContain(\"Mr. Smith.\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"splits the seed passage into sections of at most about 2400 characters\", () => {\n    const m = buildTextModel(SEED_PASSAGE.body);\n    expect(m.words.length).toBe(SEED_PASSAGE.body.trim().split(/\\s+/).length);\n    for (const s of m.sections) expect(s.text.length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(2600);\n    expect(m.words[0].charPosition).toBe(0);\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe(\"layout\", () => {\n  const model = buildTextModel(\"aa bb cc dd ee\");\n  const words = model.sections[0].words;\n\n  it(\"caps a line at words per line\", () => {\n    const lines = buildLines(words, mono, 1000, 2);\n    expect(lines.map((l) => l.words.map((w) => w.word.text))).toEqual([[\"aa\", \"bb\"], [\"cc\", \"dd\"], [\"ee\"]]);\n  });\n\n  it(\"wraps on measured width before the word cap\", () => {\n    // each word is 30px with its space; 70px fits two\n    const lines = buildLines(words, mono, 70, 6);\n    expect(lines[0].words.length).toBe(2);\n  });\n\n  it(\"groups lines into serial slides and highlight pages\", () => {\n    const lines = buildLines(words, mono, 1000, 1);\n    expect(serialChunks(lines, 2).map((c) => c.text)).toEqual([\"aa bb\", \"cc dd\", \"ee\"]);\n    const pages = highlightPages(lines, 20 + 36 * 3, 36, 1); // 3 lines per page\n    expect(pages.map((p) => p.lines.length)).toEqual([3, 2]);\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe(\"timing\", () => {\n  it(\"shortens the last chunk by how full it is\", () => {\n    expect(evenChunkDelays(1000, 3, 1, 2)).toEqual({ even: 400, last: 200 });\n  });\n\n  it(\"gives a 200 WPM single-word slide 300 ms, doubled after punctuation\", () => {\n    const m = buildTextModel(\"one two three four.\");\n    const s = m.sections[0];\n    const chunks = serialChunks(buildLines(s.words, mono, 1000, 1), 1);\n    const d = chunkDelays(s, chunks, { wpm: 200, numberOfLines: 1, speedVariability: false, punctuationPauses: true });\n    expect(d).toEqual([300, 300, 300, 600]);\n  });\n\n  it(\"formats remaining time like Spreeder\", () => {\n    expect(formatDuration(30_000)).toBe(\"Less than a minute\");\n    expect(formatDuration(3 * 60_000)).toBe(\"3 minutes\");\n    expect(formatDuration(61 * 60_000)).toBe(\"1 hour and 1 minute\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"estimates the seed at about 3 minutes at 200 WPM\", () => {\n    const m = buildTextModel(SEED_PASSAGE.body);\n    const ms = remainingMs(m, 0, { wpm: 200, groupSize: 1, numberOfLines: 1, punctuationPauses: false });\n    expect(formatDuration(ms)).toBe(\"2 minutes\");\n    expect(sectionTime(\"a b\", { wpm: 60, groupSize: 1, numberOfLines: 1, punctuationPauses: false })).toBe(2000);\n  });\n\n  it(\"reports progress by section\", () => {\n    const m = buildTextModel(\"A b. C d.\");\n    expect(progressPercent(m, 0)).toBe(0);\n  });\n});\n"}

Navigation always lands on the first word of a chunk.\n */\nexport function useSpreederEngine(args: {\n  model: TextModel;\n  prefs: Preferences;\n  stage: StageSize;\n  measure: Measure;\n  initialWord: number;\n  onFinish: () => void;\n}) {\n  const { model, prefs, stage, measure } = args;\n  const mode = prefs.reading_mode;\n  const isFlow = mode === \"flow\";\n  const isPaged = mode === \"highlight\" || mode === \"scroll\";\n  const numberOfLines = isFlow || mode === \"scroll\" ? Math.max(0, layout.chunks.length - 1) : i;\n  }, [layout, activeWord]);\n\n  // Keep the active word on a chunk start after a relayout.\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const c = layout.chunks[chunkIndex];\n    if (c && c.firstWord !== activeWord) setActiveWord(c.firstWord);\n    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps\n  }, [layout]);\n\n  const state = useRef({ layout, chunkIndex, activeWord });\n  state.current = { layout, chunkIndex, activeWord };\n  const onFinishRef = useRef(args.onFinish);\n  onFinishRef.current = args.onFinish;\n\n  const forward = useCallback(() => {\n    const { layout: l, chunkIndex: ci } = state.current;\n    if (ci + 1 < l.chunks.length) {\n      setActiveWord(l.chunks[ci + 1].firstWord);\n    } else if (l.sectionIndex + 1 < model.sections.length) {\n      setActiveWord(model.sections[l.sectionIndex + 1].words[0].index);\n    } else {\n      setPlaying(false);\n      onFinishRef.current();\n    }\n  }, [model]);\n\n  const backward = useCallback(() => {\n    const { layout: l, chunkIndex: ci } = state.current;\n    if (ci > 0) {\n      setActiveWord(l.chunks[ci - 1].firstWord);\n    } else if (l.sectionIndex > 0) {\n      const prev = layoutFor(l.sectionIndex - 1);\n      const last = prev.chunks[prev.chunks.length - 1];\n      if (last) setActiveWord(last.firstWord);\n    }\n  }, [layoutFor]);\n\n  // The play loop: each chunk stays up for its delay, then advances.\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (!playing) return;\n    const d = layout.delays[chunkIndex];\n    if (d == null) return;\n    const t = window.setTimeout(forward, d);\n    return () => window.clearTimeout(t);\n  }, [playing, layout, chunkIndex, forward]);\n\n  const play = useCallback(() => setPlaying(true), []);\n  const pause = useCallback(() => setPlaying(false), []);\n  const toggle = useCallback(() => setPlaying((p) => !p), []);\n  const next = useCallback(() => {\n    setPlaying(false);\n    forward();\n  }, [forward]);\n  const prev = useCallback(() => {\n    setPlaying(false);\n    backward();\n  }, [backward]);\n  /** Spreeder \"restart\" (R): back to the first word of the current section. */\n  const restartSection = useCallback(() => {\n    setPlaying(false);\n    const s = model.sections[state.current.layout.sectionIndex];\n    if (s) setActiveWord(s.words[0].index);\n  }, [model]);\n  const goToWord = useCallback((i: number) => setActiveWord(Math.max(0, Math.min(model.words.length - 1, i))), [model]);\n\n  // Spreeder pauses when the window loses focus.\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const onBlur = () => setPlaying(false);\n    window.addEventListener(\"blur\", onBlur);\n    return () => window.removeEventListener(\"blur\", onBlur);\n  }, []);\n\n  return {\n    activeWord,\n    playing,\n    layout,\n    chunkIndex,\n    chunk: layout.chunks[chunkIndex],\n    delay: layout.delays[chunkIndex] ?? 0,\n    play,\n    pause,\n    toggle,\n    next,\n    prev,\n    restartSection,\n    goToWord,\n  };\n}\n"}

1 : prefs.line_spacing;\n  const [hover, setHover] = useState<number | null>(null);\n\n  const wordSpan = (w: Word, x: number, y: number, color: string) => (\n    <span\n      key={w.index}\n      data-word={w.index}\n      onClick={(e) => {\n        e.stopPropagation();\n        onWordClick(w);\n      }}\n      onMouseEnter={() => setHover(w.index)}\n      onMouseLeave={() => setHover((h) => (h === w.index ? null : h))}\n      className=\"absolute cursor-pointer whitespace-pre\"\n      style={{\n        left: x,\n        top: y,\n        lineHeight: `${fs}px`,\n        color: hover === w.index ? \"#0000ff\" : color,\n        textDecoration: hover === w.index ? \"underline\" : undefined,\n      }}\n    >\n      {w.text}\n    </span>\n  );\n\n  const drawLines = (lines: Line[], top: number, xOf: (l: Line) => number, colorOf: (w: Word) => string) =>\n    lines.flatMap((l, i) => l.words.map((p) => wordSpan(p.word, xOf(l) + p.x, top + i * fs * ls, colorOf(p.word))));\n\n  let content: React.ReactNode = null;\n  if (chunk && (mode === \"serial\" || isFlow)) {\n    const S = chunk.lines.length;\n    const F = fs * S + (S - 1) * fs * (ls - 1);\n    const center = !isFlow && prefs.center_text;\n    content = drawLines(chunk.lines, (stage.height - F) / 2, (l) => (center ? (stage.width - l.width) / 2 : 20), () => \"var(--rc-read)\");\n  } else if (chunk && (mode === \"highlight\" || mode === \"scroll\")) {\n    const page = layout.pages[layout.chunkPage[chunkIndex]];\n    if (page) {\n      const inChunk = (w: Word) => w.index >= chunk.firstWord && w.index <= chunk.lastWord;\n      const lines = mode === \"scroll\" ? page.lines.filter((l) => l.words.some((p) => inChunk(p.word))) : page.lines;\n      const firstLine = page.lines.indexOf(lines[0]);\n      content = lines.flatMap((l, i) =>\n        l.words.map((p) =>\n          wordSpan(p.word, 20 + p.x, (firstLine + i) * fs * ls, mode === \"scroll\" || inChunk(p.word) ? \"var(--rc-read)\" : \"var(--rc-dim)\"),\n        ),\n      );\n    }\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <div\n      className=\"absolute inset-0 overflow-hidden\"\n      style={{ fontFamily: `${fontStack}, monospace`, fontSize: fs }}\n      onClick={onStageClick}\n    >\n      {content}\n      {isFlow && chunk && <FlowMask key={`${layout.sectionIndex}:${chunkIndex}`} duration={delay} />}\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\n/**\n * Flow reveal: a surface-coloured mask sweeps left to right across the window\n * over the chunk's display time. (Spreeder's shipped animation multiplies by the\n * per-frame delta instead of elapsed time, so it barely moves; this is the\n * intended behaviour.)\n */\nfunction FlowMask({ duration }: { duration: number }): React.ReactElement {\n  const ref = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const el = ref.current;\n    if (!el) return;\n    el.style.transition = \"none\";\n    el.style.transform = \"translateX(0)\";\n    const id = requestAnimationFrame(() => {\n      el.style.transition = `transform ${Math.max(1, duration)}ms linear`;\n      el.style.transform = `translateX(${window.innerWidth}px)`;\n    });\n    return () => cancelAnimationFrame(id);\n  }, [duration]);\n  return <div ref={ref} aria-hidden className=\"pointer-events-none absolute inset-y-0 left-0\" style={{ width: window.innerWidth, background: \"var(--rc-surface)\" }} />;\n}\n"}

 document.body,\n  );\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------- finish + toast\n\nexport function FinishAlert({ title, open, onOkay }: { title: string; open: boolean; \"rgba(0,0,0,.32)\" }}>\n      <div role=\"alertdialog\" aria-labelledby=\"rc-finish-h\" className=\"w-[280px] rounded p-5 pb-2\" style={{ background: \"var(--rc-dialog)\", color: \"var(--rc-text)\", Words turn #3e92d7 on hover and open word actions on click.\n */\nexport function EReader({\n  prefs,\n  fontStack,\n  words,\n  page,\n  onPageCount,\n  onPageWord,\n  highlight,\n  onWordClick,\n}: {\n  prefs: Preferences;\n  fontStack: string;\n  words: Word[];\n  page: number;\n  onPageCount: (n: number) => void;\n  /** First word index on the visible page (reading position). */\n  onPageWord: (i: number) => void;\n  highlight: { start: number; length: number }[];\n  onWordClick: (w: Word) => void;\n}): React.ReactElement {\n  const box = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);\n  const [width, setWidth] = useState(0);\n\n  useLayoutEffect(() => {\n    const el = box.current;\n    if (!el) return;\n    const ro = new ResizeObserver(() => setWidth(el.clientWidth));\n    ro.observe(el);\n    setWidth(el.clientWidth);\n    return () => ro.disconnect();\n  }, []);\n\n  useLayoutEffect(() => {\n    const el = box.current;\n    if (!el || width <= 0) return;\n    onPageCount(Math.max(1, Math.round((el.scrollWidth + GAP) / (width + GAP))));\n  }, [width, words, prefs.font_family, prefs.font_size, prefs.line_spacing, onPageCount]);\n\n  useLayoutEffect(() => {\n    const el = box.current;\n    if (!el || width <= 0) return;\n    el.scrollLeft = page * (width + GAP);\n    const first = [...el.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>(\"[data-word]\")].find((s) => s.offsetLeft >= page * (width + GAP) - 1);\n    if (first) onPageWord(Number(first.dataset.word));\n  }, [page, width, prefs.font_family, prefs.font_size, prefs.line_spacing, onPageWord]);\n\n  const marked = useMemo(() => {\n    const set = new Set<number>();\n    for (const h of highlight) for (let k = 0; k < h.length; k++) set.add(h.start + k);\n    return set;\n  }, [highlight]);\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"mx-auto h-full w-full max-w-[750px] px-2 pt-7\">\n      <div\n        ref={box}\n        id=\"onePage\"\n        className=\"relative overflow-hidden\"\n        onClick={(e) => {\n          const t = (e.target as HTMLElement).closest<HTMLElement>(\"[data-word]\");\n          if (t) onWordClick(words[Number(t.dataset.word)]);\n        }}\n        style={{\n          height: \"calc(100vh - 119px)\",\n          columnWidth: width > 0 ? `${width}px` : undefined,\n          columnGap: GAP,\n          columnFill: \"auto\",\n          fontFamily: fontStack,\n          fontSize: prefs.font_size,\n          lineHeight: `${prefs.font_size * prefs.line_spacing}px`,\n          color: \"var(--rc-read)\",\n        }}\n      >\n        {words.map((w) => (\n          <React.Fragment key={w.index}>\n            <span data-word={w.index} className=\"cursor-pointer hover:text-[#3e92d7]\" style={marked.has(w.index) ? { background: \"#ffff00\", color: \"#000\" } : undefined}>\n              {w.text}\n            </span>{\" \"}\n          </React.Fragment>\n        ))}\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\n/** Page of a word, measured from the rendered column layout. */\nexport function usePageOf(): (wordIndex: number) => number {\n  return useCallback((wordIndex: number) => {\n    const el = document.getElementById(\"onePage\");\n    const span = el?.querySelector<HTMLElement>(`[data-word=\"${wordIndex}\"]`);\n    if (!el || !span) return 0;\n    return Math.floor(span.offsetLeft / (el.clientWidth + GAP));\n  }, []);\n}\n\nexport function EReaderBar({ page, pageCount, onPage }: { page: number; pageCount: number; onPage: (p: number) => void }): React.ReactElement {\n  const pct = pageCount > 1 ? (page / (pageCount - 1)) * 100 : 0;\n  const track = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);\n  const fromX = (x: number) => {\n    const r = track.current!.getBoundingClientRect();\n    onPage(Math.round(Math.min(1, Math.max(0, (x - r.left) / r.width)) * (pageCount - 1)));\n  };\n  return (\n    <div className=\"flex h-10 items-center\" style={{ background: \"var(--rc-bottombar)\", borderTop: \"0.8px solid var(--rc-bottombar-border)\" }}>\n      <div\n        ref={track}\n        role=\"slider\"\n        aria-label=\"Page\"\n        aria-valuemin={1}\n        aria-valuemax={pageCount}\n        aria-valuenow={page + 1}\n        tabIndex={0}\n        className=\"relative ml-7 h-10 flex-1 cursor-pointer\"\n        onPointerDown={(e) => { (e.target as HTMLElement).setPointerCapture?.(e.pointerId); fromX(e.clientX); }}\n        onPointerMove={(e) => e.buttons === 1 && fromX(e.clientX)}\n      >\n        <div className=\"absolute left-0 right-0 top-1/2 h-1 -translate-y-1/2 rounded\" style={{ background: \"rgba(100,100,100,.4)\" }} />\n        <div className=\"absolute left-0 top-1/2 h-1 -translate-y-1/2 rounded\" style={{ width: `${pct}%`, background: \"var(--rc-slider)\" }} />\n        <span className=\"absolute top-1/2 h-[25px] w-[25px] -translate-x-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 rounded-full\" style={{ left: `${pct}%`, background: \"var(--rc-slider)\" }} />\n      </div>\n      <span className=\"ml-11 w-[60px] text-center text-sm\" style={{ color: \"var(--rc-text)\", opacity: 0.8 }}>{page + 1} / {pageCount}</span>\n      <button type=\"button\" aria-label=\"Previous page\" disabled={page <= 0} onClick={() => onPage(page - 1)} className=\"ml-3\" style={{ color: page <= 0 ? \"#bdbdbd\" : \"var(--rc-strong)\" }}>\n        <MIcon name=\"keyboard_arrow_left\" size={30} />\n      </button>\n      <button type=\"button\" aria-label=\"Next page\" disabled={page >= pageCount - 1} onClick={() => onPage(page + 1)} className=\"ml-3 mr-5\" style={{ color: page >= pageCount - 1 ? \"#bdbdbd\" : \"var(--rc-strong)\" }}>\n        <MIcon name=\"keyboard_arrow_right\" size={30} />\n      </button>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\n/** \"Search in this book\": results grouped by page, match in bold. */\nexport function EReaderSearch({\n  open,\n  onClose,\n  words,\n  pageOf,\n  onSelect,\n}: {\n  open: boolean;\n  onClose: () => void;\n  words: Word[];\n  pageOf: (i: number) => number;\n  onSelect: (page: number, hits: { start: number; length: number }[]) => void;\n}): React.ReactElement | null {\n  const [query, setQuery] = useState(\"\");\n  const [submitted, setSubmitted] = useState(\"\");\n  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<number | null>(null);\n  const qLen = submitted.trim().split(/\\s+/).filter(Boolean).length;\n  const groups = useMemo(() => {\n    const m = findMatches(words, submitted);\n    const byPage = new Map<number, number[]>();\n    for (const i of m) byPage.set(pageOf(i), [...(byPage.get(pageOf(i)) ?? []), i]);\n    return [...byPage.entries()].sort((a, b) => a[0] - b[0]);\n  }, [words, submitted, pageOf]);\n  const total = groups.reduce((n, g) => n + g[1].length, 0);\n  if (!open) return null;\n  const pick = (page: number, starts: number[]) => {\n    setSelected(page);\n    onSelect(page, starts.map((s) => ({ start: s, length: qLen })));\n  };\n  return (\n    <div className=\"fixed bottom-5 right-[30px] top-[50px] z-40 flex w-[350px] flex-col rounded-xl p-[34px] pt-5\" style={{ background: \"var(--rc-dialog)\", color: \"var(--rc-text)\", boxShadow: \"0 4px 18px rgba(0,0,0,.25)\" }}>\n      <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between\">\n        <h3 className=\"text-2xl\">Search</h3>\n        <button type=\"button\" aria-label=\"Close search\" onClick={onClose} style={{ color: \"var(--rc-strong)\" }}><MIcon name=\"close\" /></button>\n      </div>\n      <form\n        className=\"relative mt-7\"\n        onSubmit={(e) => {\n          e.preventDefault();\n          setSubmitted(query);\n          setSelected(null);\n        }}\n      >\n        <label className=\"block text-[11px]\" style={{ color: \"#1a62f2\" }}>Search in this book</label>\n        <input value={query} onChange={(e) => setQuery(e.target.value)} aria-label=\"Search in this book\" autoFocus className=\"w-full bg-transparent pb-1 pr-6 text-sm outline-none\" style={{ borderBottom: \"2px solid #1a62f2\", color: \"var(--rc-strong)\" }} />\n        {query && (\n          <button type=\"button\" aria-label=\"Clear\" onClick={() => { setQuery(\"\"); setSubmitted(\"\"); }} className=\"absolute bottom-1.5 right-0\" style={{ color: \"#757575\" }}>\n            <MIcon name=\"close\" size={16} />\n          </button>\n        )}\n      </form>\n      {submitted && <p className=\"mt-4 text-sm\" style={{ color: \"#757575\" }}>{total} results in book</p>}\n      <div className=\"mt-7 min-h-0 flex-1 space-y-2 overflow-y-auto\">\n        {groups.map(([page, starts]) => {\n          const s = starts[0];\n          return (\n            <button key={page} type=\"button\" onClick={() => pick(page, starts)} className=\"flex w-full gap-2 rounded-xl px-2.5 py-3 text-left\" style={{ background: selected === page ? \"#d5e7fb\" : \"transparent\" }}>\n              <Icon name=\"search\" size={20} style={{ color: selected === page ? \"#1a62f2\" : \"#757575\" }} />\n              <span>\n                <span className=\"block text-sm\" style={{ color: \"#757575\" }}>Page {page + 1} ({starts.length} results)</span>\n                <span className=\"block text-sm\" style={{ color: selected === page ? \"#212121\" : \"var(--rc-strong)\" }}>\n                  {words[s - 1]?.text} <strong>{words.slice(s, s + qLen).map((w) => w.text).join(\" \")}</strong> {words.slice(s + qLen, s + qLen + 2).map((w) => w.text).join(\" \")}\n                </span>\n              </span>\n            </button>\n          );\n        })}\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"}

height=\"60\" aria-hidden><circle cx=\"30\" cy=\"30\" r=\"27\" fill=\"none\" stroke=\"currentColor\" strokeWidth=\"5\" /><path d=\"M24 18 L42 30 L24 42 Z\" fill=\"currentColor\" /></svg>\n            </TransportButton>\n            <TransportButton label=\"Next\" onClick={engine.next}>\n              <svg viewBox=\"0 0 50 50\" width=\"50\" height=\"50\" aria-hidden><path d=\"M17 13 L35 25 L17 37 Z\" fill=\"none\" stroke=\"currentColor\" strokeWidth=\"4\" strokeLinejoin=\"round\" /></svg>\n            </TransportButton>\n          </div>\n        )}\n      </main>\n\n      {/* Bottom bar */}\n      {isEReader ? (\n        <EReaderBar page={page} pageCount={pageCount} onPage={goPage} />\n      ) : (\n        <footer className=\"grid h-10 shrink-0 grid-cols-3 items-center px-5\" style={{ background: \"var(--rc-bottombar)\", borderTop: \"0.8px solid var(--rc-bottombar-border)\" }}>\n          <div className=\"flex justify-center\">\n            <button ref={kbdBtn} type=\"button\" aria-label=\"Keyboard shortcuts\" onClick={() => setKbdOpen((o) => !o)} className=\"flex h-8 w-10 items-center justify-center\" style={{ color: \"var(--rc-text)\" }}>\n              <Icon name=\"keypad\" size={22} />\n            </button>\n          </div>\n          <div className=\"flex items-center justify-center gap-[19px]\">\n            <BarButton onClick={() => { engine.pause(); setWpmOpen(true); }} icon=\"speedometer\">{prefs.wpm}</BarButton>\n            <BarButton onClick={() => { engine.pause(); setViewOpen(true); }} icon=\"list\">View</BarButton>\n          </div>\n          <div className=\"pl-[100px] text-sm leading-[31px]\" style={{ color: \"var(--rc-text)\", opacity: 0.8 }}>\n            {pct}% - {formatDuration(remaining)} remaining\n          </div>\n        </footer>\n      )}\n\n      <ShortcutsPopover open={kbdOpen} anchorX={kbdBtn.current?.getBoundingClientRect().left ?? 230} onClose={() => setKbdOpen(false)} />\n      <PreferencesDialog open={prefsOpen} onClose={() => setPrefsOpen(false)} prefs={prefs} onChange={updatePrefs} name={name} />\n      <WpmDialog open={wpmOpen} onClose={() => setWpmOpen(false)} value={prefs.wpm} onSave={changeSpeed} />\n      <ViewPanel\n        open={viewOpen}\n        onClose={() => setViewOpen(false)}\n        title={passage.title}\n        words={model.words}\n        currentWord={activeWord}\n        footer={`${formatDuration(remaining)} to go - ${prefs.wpm} WPM`}\n        onJump={(w) => engine.goToWord(w.index)}\n        onPlay={engine.play}\n      />\n      <BookmarksDialog\n        open={bmOpen}\n        onClose={() => setBmOpen(false)}\n        bookmarks={bookmarks.data ?? []}\n        words={model.words}\n        onOpenBookmark={(b: Bookmark) => { setBmOpen(false); setNote({ mode: \"edit\", word: model.words[b.word_index], bookmark: b }); }}\n      />\n      <WordActionSheet word={sheetWord} onClose={() => setSheetWord(null)} onBookmark={openBookmarkFor} />\n      <BookmarkNoteDialog\n        state={note}\n        title={passage.title}\n        onClose={() => setNote(null)}\n        onSave={(text) => {\n          if (note?.mode === \"edit\") updateBookmark.mutate({ id: note.bookmark.id, note: text || null });\n          else if (note) addBookmark.mutate({ passage_id: passage.id, word_index: note.word.index, note: text || null });\n          setNote(null);\n        }}\n        onDelete={(b) => { deleteBookmark.mutate({ id: b.id, passage_id: passage.id }); setNote(null); }}\n        onGoTo={(w) => { engine.goToWord(w.index); if (isEReader) setPage(pageOf(w.index)); setNote(null); }}\n      />\n      {isEReader && (\n        <EReaderSearch open={searchOpen} onClose={() => { setSearchOpen(false); setMarks([]); }} words={model.words} pageOf={pageOf} onSelect={(p, hits) => { setPage(p); setMarks(hits); }} />\n      )}\n      <FinishAlert\n        open={finished}\n        title={passage.title}\n        onOkay={() => {\n          saveProgress.mutate({ passage_id: passage.id, word_index: 0, completed: true });\n          if (document.fullscreenElement) document.exitFullscreen();\n          navigate(RC_LIBRARY_PATH);\n        }}\n      />\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction TransportButton({ label, onClick, children, big = false }: { label: string; onClick: () => void; children: React.ReactNode; big?: boolean }): React.ReactElement {\n  return (\n    <button\n      type=\"button\"\n      aria-label={label}\n      onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); onClick(); }}\n      className=\"pointer-events-auto flex items-center justify-center rounded-full transition-colors hover:bg-[rgba(128,128,128,.18)]\"\n      style={{ width: big ? 64 : 54, height: big ? 64 : 54, color: \"var(--rc-text)\" }}\n    >\n      {children}\n    </button>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction BarButton({ onClick, icon, children }: { onClick: () => void; icon: \"speedometer\" | \"list\"; children: React.ReactNode }): React.ReactElement {\n  return (\n    <button type=\"button\" onClick={onClick} className=\"flex h-[30px] w-20 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded text-[13px] font-medium\" style={{ color: \"var(--rc-blue)\", border: \"0.8px solid var(--rc-bottombar-border)\" }}>\n      <Icon name={icon} size={20} />\n      {children}\n    </button>\n  );\n}\n"}

text-red-500\">{error}</p>}\n        <div className=\"mt-8 flex items-center justify-end gap-6 pr-6\">\n          {mode !== \"edit\" && (\n            <>\n              <input ref={file} type=\"file\" {"file_path": "C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os-projects/reading-center/src/features/reading-center/lib/goals.ts", "content": "/**\n * Spreeder goal maths (recommended-goals-result + reading-test, shipped bundle).\n */\n\n/** Reading test speed: floor(60 * words / seconds), rounded to 10. */\nexport function readingTestWpm(words: number, seconds: number): number {\n  if (seconds <= 0) return 0;\n  return 10 * Math.round(Math.floor((60 * words) / seconds) / 10);\n}\n\n/** Recommended speed goal for a measured speed, on a scale whose max is `max` WPM. */\nexport function recommendedGoal(speed: number, max = 2000): number {\n  const L = max;\n  const S = speed;\n  let z: number;\n  if (S <= 0.01 * L) z = 0.05 * L;\n  else if (S <= 0.025 * L) z = 0.05 * L + ((0.05 * L) / (0.015 * L)) * (S - 0.01 * L);\n  else if (S <= 0.1 * L) z = 0.1 * L + ((0.2 * L) / (0.075 * L)) * (S - 0.025 * L);\n  else if (S <= 0.25 * L) z = 0.3 * L + ((0.2 * L) / (0.15 * L)) * (S - 0.1 * L);\n  else if (S <= 0.5 * L) z = 0.5 * L + (S - 0.25 * L);\n  else if (S <= 0.8 * L) z = 0.75 * L + ((0.25 * L) / (0.3 * L)) * (S - 0.5 * L);\n  else z = L;\n  return 10 * Math.round(Math.min(z, L) / 10);\n}\n"}

{"file_path": "C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os-projects/reading-center/src/features/reading-center/components/SpeedGauge.tsx", "content": "import React from \"react\";\n\n/**\n * Speedometer dial like Spreeder's Vital Stats gauges (0 to 2000): white face,\n * green arc, red needle, blue hub, value on the dial. Drawn from the live\n * screenshot; the original artwork was not extracted.\n */\nexport function SpeedGauge({ value, max = 2000, size = 84 }: { value: number; max?: number; size?: number }): React.ReactElement {\n  const start = 135; // degrees, 0 at 3 o'clock, clockwise\n  const sweep = 270;\n  const pt = (deg: number, r: number) => {\n    const a = (deg * Math.PI) / 180;\n    return [50 + r * Math.cos(a), 50 + r * Math.sin(a)];\n  };\n  const arc = (r: number, from: number, to: number) => {\n    const [x1, y1] = pt(from, r);\n    const [x2, y2] = pt(to, r);\n    return `M ${x1} ${y1} A ${r} ${r} 0 ${to - from > 180 ? 1 : 0} 1 ${x2} ${y2}`;\n  };\n  const ratio = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, value / max));\n  const needle = start + sweep * ratio;\n  const [nx, ny] = pt(needle, 30);\n  return (\n    <svg viewBox=\"0 0 100 100\" width={size} height={size} role=\"img\" aria-label={`${value} words per minute`}>\n      <defs>\n        <radialGradient id=\"rc-bezel\" cx=\"50%\" cy=\"40%\" r=\"60%\">\n          <stop offset=\"0%\" stopColor=\"#f5f5f5\" />\n          <stop offset=\"100%\" stopColor=\"#9e9e9e\" />\n        </radialGradient>\n      </defs>\n      <circle cx=\"50\" cy=\"50\" r=\"48\" fill=\"url(#rc-bezel)\" />\n      <circle cx=\"50\" cy=\"50\" r=\"42\" fill=\"#ffffff\" />\n      <path d={arc(36, start, start + sweep)} fill=\"none\" stroke=\"#2e9a2e\" strokeWidth=\"7\" />\n      {Array.from({ length: 11 }, (_, i) => {\n        const d = start + (sweep * i) / 10;\n        const [x1, y1] = pt(d, 31);\n        const [x2, y2] = pt(d, 27);\n        return <line key={i} x1={x1} y1={y1} x2={x2} y2={y2} stroke=\"#616161\" strokeWidth=\"1\" />;\n      })}\n      <line x1=\"50\" y1=\"50\" x2={nx} y2={ny} stroke=\"#e53935\" strokeWidth=\"3\" strokeLinecap=\"round\" />\n      <circle cx=\"50\" cy=\"50\" r=\"5\" fill=\"#3e92d7\" />\n      <text x=\"50\" y=\"78\" textAnchor=\"middle\" fontSize=\"9\" fill=\"#424242\" fontFamily=\"Poppins, sans-serif\">{value}</text>\n    </svg>\n  );\n}\n"}

Your results will appear here later <span className=\"ml-1.5 text-2xl\" aria-hidden>🙂</span>\n  </div>\n);\n\nfunction Card({ title, children, period, onPeriod, onRefresh }: { title: string; children: React.ReactNode; period?: string; onPeriod?: (p: string) => void; onRefresh?: () => void }): React.ReactElement {\n  return (\n    <section className=\"flex h-[230px] flex-col rounded-[15px] p-2\" style={{ background: \"var(--rc-card)\" }}>\n      <div className=\"flex h-[38px] items-center justify-between px-2\">\n        <h6 className=\"text-lg\">{title}</h6>\n        {onPeriod && period && (\n          <div className=\"flex items-center gap-2\">\n            <button type=\"button\" aria-label={`Refresh ${title}`} onClick={onRefresh} className=\"flex\"><Icon name=\"refresh\" size={22} /></button>\n            <Dropdown ariaLabel={`${title} period`} value={period} options={STAT_PERIODS.map((p) => ({ value: p.value, label: p.value === \"6m\" ? \"6 months\" : p.label }))} onChange={(v) => onPeriod(String(v))} buttonStyle={{ border: \"none\", padding: \"0 0 0 8px\", fontSize: 16, fontWeight: 400, color: \"var(--rc-text)\" }} />\n          </div>\n        )}\n      </div>\n      <div className=\"min-h-0 flex-1\">{children}</div>\n    </section>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction MetricCard({ title, metric, initial }: { title: string; metric: Metric; initial: string }): React.ReactElement {\n  const [period, setPeriod] = useState(initial);\n  const q = useSessions(useSince(period));\n  const data = series(q.data ?? <Modal open onClose={onClose} fullScreen labelledBy=\"rc-rec-title\">\n      <div className=\"flex h-full flex-col px-[34px] pt-5\" style={{ background: \"var(--rc-page)\" }}>\n        <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between\">\n          <h2 id=\"rc-rec-title\" className=\"text-xl\">Goal Recommendation Test</h2>\n          <button type=\"button\" aria-label=\"Close\" onClick={onClose}><MIcon name=\"close\" /></button>\n        </div>\n        {step === \"intro\" && (\n          <div className=\"flex flex-1 flex-col items-center justify-center pb-24 text-center\">\n            <Icon name=\"flag\" size={84} style={{ color: blue }} />\n            <h3 className=\"mt-6 text-[44px] leading-tight\" style={{ color: blue }}>Recommend Goals</h3>\n            <p className=\"mt-5 text-lg font-medium\">Please complete the following test and the Reading Center will recommend your ideal goals.</p>\n            <button type=\"button\" disabled={!passages.length} className=\"mt-8 h-[60px] w-[200px] rounded text-base font-medium text-white disabled:opacity-40\" style={{ background: \"#2c7a0b\" }} onClick={() => setStep(\"pick\")}>Start</button>\n          </div>\n        )}\n        {step === \"pick\" && (\n          <div className=\"flex flex-1 flex-col items-center justify-center gap-6 pb-24\">\n            <p className=\"text-lg\">Choose a text to read at your normal pace.</p>\n            <Dropdown ariaLabel=\"Test text\" minWidth={320} value={passage?.id ?? \"\"} options={passages.map((p) => ({ value: p.id, label: p.title }))} onChange={(v) => setPassageId(String(v))} />\n            <button type=\"button\" className=\"rc-green-btn w-[200px]\" onClick={() => { setStartedAt(Date.now()); pb-5\">\n            <div className=\"mt-4 min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-y-auto rounded-[10px] p-6 text-lg leading-8\" style={{ background: \"var(--rc-surface)\", color: \"var(--rc-read)\" }} onScroll={(e) => { const t = e.currentTarget; if (Math.round(t.scrollTop) >= t.scrollHeight - t.offsetHeight - 1) setCanFinish(true); }} ref={(el) => { if (el && el.scrollHeight <= el.clientHeight + 1 && !canFinish) setCanFinish(true); }}>\n              {buildTextModel(passage.body).words.map((w) => w.text).join(\" \")}\n            </div>\n            <div className=\"mt-4 flex justify-end\">\n </Modal>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction EditProfile({ name, onClose, onSave }: { name: string; onClose: () => void; onSave: (n: string) => void }): React.ReactElement {\n  const [v, setV] = useState(name);\n {"file_path": "C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os-projects/reading-center/src/features/reading-center/lib/workerTimer.ts", "content": "/**\n * Timer in a Web Worker, as Spreeder does it: browsers clamp setTimeout in\n * background or hidden tabs to once a second, which would freeze reading at\n * 60 WPM. Worker timers are not clamped. Falls back to setTimeout.\n */\nconst SOURCE = `\nlet id = null;\nself.onmessage = (e) => {\n  if (id !== null) { clearTimeout(id); id = null; }\n  if (e.data && e.data.delay != null) {\n    const start = Date.now();\n    const tick = () => {\n      if (Date.now() - start >= e.data.delay) { id = null; self.postMessage(e.data.token); }\n      else id = setTimeout(tick, Math.min(4, e.data.delay));\n    };\n    id = setTimeout(tick, Math.max(0, e.data.delay - 4));\n  }\n};`;\n\nlet worker: Worker | null | undefined;\nlet nextToken = 1;\nconst callbacks = new Map<number, () => void>();\n\nfunction getWorker(): Worker | null {\n  if (worker !== undefined) return worker;\n  try {\n    worker = new Worker(URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([SOURCE], { type: \"text/javascript\" })));\n    worker.onmessage = (e: MessageEvent<number>) => {\n      const cb = callbacks.get(e.data);\n      callbacks.delete(e.data);\n      cb?.();\n    };\n  } catch {\n    worker = null;\n  }\n  return worker;\n}\n\n/** One pending timer at a time (the reader only ever needs one). Returns a cancel function. */\nexport function startTimer(delay: number, cb: () => void): () => void {\n  const w = getWorker();\n  if (!w) {\n    const t = window.setTimeout(cb, delay);\n    return () => window.clearTimeout(t);\n  }\n  const token = nextToken++;\n  callbacks.clear();\n  callbacks.set(token, cb);\n  w.postMessage({ delay, token });\n  return () => {\n    if (callbacks.delete(token)) w.postMessage({ delay: null });\n  };\n}\n"}

## assistant

Reading Center is rebuilt to copy Spreeder's look and behaviour, and it's running at http://localhost:8100. It follows Spreeder's actual code and live screens, not my earlier guesses. Typecheck passes, 28/28 tests pass, the production build succeeds, and it's committed as `441536b`.

**How I matched it**
- **Live capture:** I went through Spreeder screen by screen in your logged-in Chrome, recording exact colours, fonts and sizes in all four themes.
- **Code analysis:** I downloaded the app's full code (the main bundle plus 219 lazy-loaded chunks) and ran it through REA. REA's server didn't connect this session, so I used its command-line version.
- **Spec:** everything is written up in [research/2026-10-13-spreeder-reconstruction-spec.md](research/2026-10-13-spreeder-reconstruction-spec.md).

**What I got wrong before, now fixed**
- Colours and font: Spreeder's real palette and Poppins replace LSAT U's grey look, across Light, Dark, High Contrast (charcoal, not black and yellow) and System.
- Flow: it reveals each chunk left to right; it doesn't flash chunks across a line.
- Highlight: the current word isn't bold.
- Speed Variability: it goes by chunk size, not word length.
- Punctuation pauses: they apply at every group size.
- Resume: there's no "pick up where you left off" prompt; it just resumes.
- R: it restarts the current section, not the whole passage.
- Recommend: it's a reading test; I had made up a "3× your speed" formula.

**The reader now works like Spreeder's**
- The timing formula is Spreeder's: at 200 WPM a word shows for about 310 ms, and punctuated words stay up twice as long.
- It uses the same timer approach, so reading doesn't slow down in a background tab.
- Clicking the reading area pauses or resumes. Clicking a word opens Bookmark/Note.
- The View panel and e-Reader search work as in Spreeder. The same passage even paginates to the same "1 / 6" pages.

**Checked in the browser**
- Library
- Serial playback and timing
- Preferences dialog
- Highlight mode
- e-Reader with search
- View panel
- Light and Dark themes

Not clicked through yet: Flow, Scroll, the Profile page, the Add Text screens and the card-menu dialogs.

**Where I deliberately differ from Spreeder**
- **Flow:** Spreeder's own Flow is broken. Its wipe uses the time between frames instead of elapsed time, so it shows almost nothing. I built the reveal it was clearly meant to do, since you said every feature should work.
- **Branding:** no Spreeder logo, cover art or their private icon font. Matching open-source icons stand in.

**Not built yet**
- **Need an outside service:** Look Up Word, Add to Wordela Word List, AI Book Summary, AI Generate, Spreeder's 20,504-book catalogue, Courses content, and sharing.
- **Built from my best guess, because I couldn't see them live:** the Tag, Edit Image, Delete and Edit Profile dialogs; the filled bookmarks list; the charts once there's data; the Light-theme sidebar colours; and the speedometer artwork. Seeing these would have meant creating or deleting things in your Spreeder account, so I left them.

**Your Spreeder account:** I switched it to Light, High Contrast and the other reading modes to capture them, then set it back to Dark and Serial. Opening the passage and briefly playing Scroll moved its saved position forward a few words. Nothing else changed: no bookmarks, no goal change, no text added or deleted.

**Before LSAT U integration:** the LSAT U branch's copy of the feature is now well behind this rebuild. Integrating means porting the standalone version across, not merging the two.

## assistant

Flow, Scroll and Profile now all work. Testing turned up three bugs, now fixed and committed as `e41d2c2`; all 28 tests pass. One thing I couldn't see: the browser pane was hidden, so I never watched Flow's sweep move on screen. I confirmed it from the browser's animation state instead.

**Bugs found and fixed**
- **Blank reader in a background tab.** The reader drew no words and couldn't be played. Its stage size came only from a resize listener that doesn't fire while a tab is hidden. It now measures itself as soon as it opens.
- **Flow's wipe could skip.** When no frame was drawn in between, the mask jumped straight to the end instead of sweeping. It now sweeps every time.
- **Empty Profile charts in a background tab.** The axes showed but the bars and line stayed at zero, because the entrance animation never ran. The charts now draw without it.

**Flow**
- Each chunk appears left-aligned, and the next one comes up after its proper time (about 900 ms for 3 words at 200 WPM).
- The browser confirms a running 894 ms sweep across the chunk.

**Scroll**
- Only the current line shows.
- Each line appears 36px lower than the last.
- After 15 lines it starts again at the top of the page, which is Spreeder's rule for 36px text in this window.

**Profile**
- **Empty dashboard:** both gauges show (0 and 600), and the three chart cards say "Nothing just yet".
- **Dashboard with sample sessions:** all three charts draw, and Current Speed shows 240, which is correct for the sample data.
- **Stats tab:** the metric and period pickers match Spreeder's options. Switching to 24 Hours narrows the chart to today.
- **Options menu:** Theme switches between Light, Dark, High Contrast and System, and Edit Profile renames the heading.
- **Goals:** the dialog text matches Spreeder's. The slider and Set update the Target Speed gauge.
- **Recommend:** the test runs from intro to text to reading. Done stays disabled until you scroll to the end, then it shows your speed, a recommended goal, and Set goals saves it. My test finished in about a second, so the speed came out absurdly high and the goal capped at 2,000, as Spreeder's formula does.
- **Settings:** opens with only Appearance and Advanced, and the right defaults.

## Current AI OS execution contract

Use the host-supported time/file tools, preserve current open state and explicit scope. Chat-only continuity remains distinct from file-writing wrapup or a broad system audit.

# Session Handoff

Produce a repeatable end-of-session summary so Joshua can `/clear` and start a fresh agent without losing continuity. The next agent should pick up by reading this summary alone.

This is a **context-handoff artifact**, not a status report. The audience is a future instance of you, not a stakeholder.

**Handoff vs. wrapup:** run `session-handoff` when the *context window* is the problem — nearing ~250k tokens (the dumb zone), or passing work between Claude and Codex. It is chat-only and writes nothing. Run `wrapup` when the *work day* is done — it strikes tasks, refreshes `wiki/hot.md`, and appends `wiki/log.md`. They can be run back to back (wrapup to persist, then handoff to reset).

## When to invoke

Joshua says: "session handoff", "hand off", "handoff summary", "summarize before I clear", or any near-equivalent. Note "let's wrap up" belongs to `wrapup`, not here — if he says that and the context window is also deep, run `wrapup` first, then offer this. Also invoke proactively if he says he's about to `/clear` without having run it, or if the session is clearly deep into the dumb zone.

## How to produce the summary

1. **Review the full conversation**, not just the last few turns. Handoffs miss things when they only summarize recent context.
2. **Pull state from these sources (in order):**
   - Plan files referenced this session (check `~/.claude/plans/` if a plan was mentioned).
   - TodoWrite state — any in-progress or pending tasks.
   - Background processes started with `run_in_background` — shell IDs are load-bearing for the next agent.
   - Files created or modified this session — you know what you touched; don't grep to re-discover.
   - Memory files written or updated (`~/.claude/projects/C--Users-Joshu-dev-ai-os/memory/`).
   - Unresolved questions — things you asked Joshua that never got a clear answer, or things he asked that got deflected.
3. **Do NOT audit the filesystem.** This is synthesis of what happened in THIS session. No `git log`, no broad `Glob` sweeps. If you didn't touch it this session, it doesn't belong here.
4. **Produce the output in chat.** Do not write a file. Do not update memory. Chat-only.

## Output template — use exactly this structure, every time

```
# Session Handoff — <one-line title of what this session was about>

## Where it started
<2-3 sentences: what Joshua asked for, key framing or constraints that emerged>

## Decisions locked + what shipped
- <decision or change> — <why, and where it lives (absolute path if a file)>
- ...

## Key files for next session
- `<absolute path>` — <why the next agent should read this first>
- Plan file: `<path>` (if a plan drove the session)
- Memory files touched: `<paths>` (if any)

## Running state
- Background processes: <shell IDs + what they are + how to kill> — or "none"
- Dev servers / ports: <url + port> — or "none"
- Open worktrees / branches: <paths> — or "none"

## Verified state as of <HH:MM>
- <claim about live/system state> — <how it was checked, and when>
- <claim> — NOT re-verified this session; last confirmed <when>
- ...

## Verification — how to confirm things still work
- `<command>` — <expected outcome>
- ...

## Deferred + open questions
- Deferred: <item> — <why pushed to later>
- OPEN (must be resolved or re-asked before new work): <question needing Joshua's input> — <context>

## Pick up here
<1-2 sentences: the single most likely next action for a fresh agent>
```

## Hard rules

1. **Chat output only.** Never write the handoff to a file. Never update memory from this skill. (That is `wrapup`'s job, not this one.)
2. **Never invent state.** If a section has nothing to report, write "none" — do not omit the section. Structure stability is the whole point.
3. **Absolute paths always.** The next agent may have a different working directory, or be Codex.
4. **If a plan file drove the session, name it first** in "Key files" so the next agent reads it before anything else.
5. **No emojis, no hype, no "great job" summaries.** Terse and concrete — paths, commands, shell IDs, decisions. Tone of a seasoned engineer handing off at end-of-shift.
6. **Background process IDs are critical.** If you started any `run_in_background` shells, their IDs must appear in "Running state" with the kill command — the next agent cannot find them otherwise.
7. **Stamp verified state, and separate it from assumed state.** Run `date "+%H:%M"` and use the real time. Every claim about live systems (deploy status, DB contents, what was sent, what is published) is either something you actually checked this session — say how — or explicitly marked NOT re-verified. A handoff that presents stale belief as current state makes the next session re-derive everything, which is the exact cost this skill exists to remove.
8. **Open questions are obligations, not notes.** Anything under OPEN must be resolved by the next session or re-asked to Joshua before it starts new work. Requirements have been silently re-typed across sessions, and a promised artifact once never arrived because nobody re-asked. If a question was open in the handoff you are picking up from, it is the first thing you raise.

## Anti-patterns — do not do these

- Summarizing the last 3 turns and calling it a handoff.
- Listing files by relative path.
- Skipping "Running state" because "nothing is running" — write "none" instead.
- Writing the summary to any file. This is chat-only by design.
- Adding a "what went well / what went poorly" retrospective. This isn't a retro.
- Recommending next steps beyond the single "Pick up here" line. The next agent decides; you just hand off.

  - whitespace collapsed, no paragraphs
  - sentence sections of about 2,000–2,400 characters
  - line building by measured width and Words Per Line

- **Worker timer** (`lib/workerTimer.ts`). Like Spreeder, it uses a Web Worker because `setTimeout` is clamped to 1 s in hidden tabs.
- **Flow differs from Spreeder on purpose.** Spreeder's mask uses `timeDiff/m`, which is buggy and shows almost nothing. Ours is the intended left-to-right reveal.
- **Brand assets not copied.** No Spreeder logo, cover art or icon font. Ionicons (npm `ionicons@7.4.0`) and Material Icons via Google Fonts stand in.
- **Goal recommendation uses Spreeder's real logic** (`lib/goals.ts`): reading-test WPM, then a piecewise curve with max 2000.
- **Not built** (need a service): Look Up Word, Add to Wordela Word List, AI Book Summary, AI Generate, the 20,504-book catalogue, Courses content, share, Sign Out.
- **Built from inference** (not observed in Spreeder): the filled bookmark list, the Tag, Edit Image, Delete and Edit Profile dialogs, the reading-test text picker, charts with data, the Light-theme sidenav colours, the speedometer artwork.
